Algorithm:
Given p (sum1), for iter:
  c=conv(p,p)
  w=softmax(β c - β max) / maybe use top-k? For LSE, gradient g=2 conv(p,rev(w)).
  Mirror step: log p_new = log p - η g. But p can become small. Project to nonnegative and renormalize.
  Line search choose η to minimize exact max or LSE.

We can design a more powerful algorithm:
1. Use **surrogate objective in scaled d** to stabilize beta.
2. Use **iterative reweighted active-set (IRLS)** on d = n c. Since LSE approximates max, weights w=exp(β(d-M+1)). We can perform **projected gradient in log space with backtracking**.
3. Use **continuation** β from ~2 to ~1000, with multiple restarts.
4. Use **exact active-set annealing**: At each iteration, maintain soft weights but probably top.
5. Use **line search with exact score** and **momentum/Adam** to reduce oscillations.
6. Use **population**: parent + alternative lengths; each candidate times.

Let's implement top-level functions:

```
def next_pow2(m): return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()

def conv_full(a, b):
   if a is None? use one arg.
   M = len(a)+len(b)-1; N=next_pow2(M); fa=rfft(a,N); fb=rfft(b,N); return irfft(fa*fb,N)[:M]
```
For self-convolution, if b same, use fa*fa.

For gradient h = corr(p,w): use conv_full(p[::-1], w)[:?] slice? We need length n. Let's write:
```
def conv_corr_p_w(p, w):
    # returns array length n: h[j]=sum_i p[i] w[i+j]
    # using conv(p_rev, w)
    p_rev = p[::-1]
    full = conv_full(p_rev, w)
    return full[n-1:2*n-1]
```

```
def lbfgs_jac_z(z, beta, n):

    score, gp = smooth_score_grad_p(p,beta)
    gx = p*(gp - dot(p,gp))
    return gx[:n-1]
```

```
def raw_scaled_lse(a, beta):
  n=len(a); S=a.sum(); if S<=1e-9: return 1e10, zeros
  c=fast_conv(a); d=n*c/(S*S)
  dmax=d.max(); z=beta*(d-dmax); e=exp(z); w=e/e.sum(); L=dmax + log(e.sum())/beta
  score=2*L
  h=corr(a,w)
  LDS=float(np.dot(w,d)) # weighted sum d
  grad= (4.0*n*h)/(S*S) - (4.0*LDS)/S
  return score, grad
```

```
def corr_exp(a,w):
   n=len(a)
   M=3*n-2
   N=next_pow2(M)
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], N)
   fw=np.fft.rfft(w, N)
   return np.fft.irfft(fa*fw, N)[n-1:2*n-1]
```

```
def fast_self_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n=len(a); M=2*n-1; N=next_power_of_two(M)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
```

```
def lse_score_grad_tuple(a,beta):
    n=a.size; grad=np.zeros(n)
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-8: return 1e10, grad
    c=fast_self_conv(a)
    d=(float(n)*c)/(S*S)
    dmax=float(d.max())
    if not np.isfinite(dmax) or dmax <= 0: return 1e10, grad
    z=beta*(d-dmax)
    e=np.exp(z)
    sume=float(e.sum())
    L=dmax + np.log(sume)/beta
    LDS = dmax + np.log(sume)? No LDS=sum e*d/sum e. Compute `LDS = float(np.dot(e,d)/sum_e)`. Not equal to L. Need for constant term. For LSE, LDS = sum w d (weighted average). We need this for gradient constant. Use e.
    h = corr_pair(a, e) # no need normalize? w=e/sum_e. In grad first term uses w; if use e unnormalized, first term w and LDS. So divide by sum_e or incorporate.
    h = h / sum_e
    LDS = dot(e,d)/sum_e
    score=2*L + PEN*(S-1)^2
    grad=4*n*h/(S*S) - (4*LDS)/S + 2*PEN*(S-1)
```

`topk_score_grad_tuple(a, k)`:
```
n; S; d
m=2n-1; k=int(k); if k>m: k=m
if k<=0: k=1
# Use np.partition
if k==m:
   w = np.ones(m)/m; L=d.mean()
else:
   # get threshold: (m-k)th smallest in partition; top k are indices >= m-k.
   # Need avoid modifying? np.partition returns copy.
   part = np.argpartition(d, m-k)[m-k:]  # top k indices unsorted
   thresh = float(d[part].min())
   if k > 1:
       below = float(np.partition(d, m-k-1)[m-k-1])
   else:
       below = thresh - 0.05*max(thresh,1e-8)
   gap = thresh - below
   # width = max(gap, 0.02*dmax, 1e-9)
   dmax=d.max()
   width = max(gap, 0.02*dmax, 1e-10)
   # logistic gamma maybe 6 or 10
   inv = 6*(d-thresh)/width
   # avoid overflow; d-thresh up to width? can be large; clip inv [-50,50]
   w = 1.0/(1.0+np.exp(-np.clip(inv,-50,50)))
   sw=float(w.sum())
   if sw < 1.0:
        w = np.zeros(m); w[part]=1.0; w*=1.0/k; sw=1.0
   else:
        w /= sw
   L=float(np.dot(w,d))
score=2*L + PEN*(S-1)^2
h = corr_pair(a,w) # w normalized
LDS=L
grad=4*n*h/(S*S) -4*LDS/S + 2*PEN*(S-1)
```

`subgrad_polish_tuple(a, max_iters, deadline)`:
Top-level no closure:
```
def subgrad_polish(a, max_iters=30, deadline=None):
    a=normalize_a(a.copy()); best_a=a; best_sc=exact_score_a(a); cur_sc=best_sc
    n=len(a)
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if deadline is not None and time.monotonic()>deadline: break
       c=fast_self_conv(a); S=a.sum(); if S<... break
       d=(n*c)/(S*S); dmax=d.max()
       if not finite: break
       e=np.exp(300*(d-dmax)); e/=e.sum()
       h=corr_pair(a,e)
       hmax=float(h.max())
       if hmax < 1e-14: break
       cands=[]
       base=1.0/hmax
       for mult in [0.001,0.01,0.05,0.1,0.3,1.0]:
           eta=mult*base
           p=a*np.exp(-eta*h)
           p=np.clip(p,0,None); S2=p.sum()
           if S2<1e-9: continue
           p*= (1.0/S2)  # target sum 1
           sc=exact_score_a(p)
           if sc < best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_a=p.copy()
           if sc < cur_sc: cur_a=p; cur_sc=sc # need top-level? variables local okay, no closure.
       if cur_a is None: break
       a=cur_a
       if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
    return best_sc,best_a
```

```
def generate_inits(n, rng, parent):
    inits=[]
    if parent is not None and parent.size>0:
       pr=resample_sequence(parent,n)
       add_pos(inits, pr)
       add_pos(inits, pr[::-1])
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    add_pos(inits, np.ones(n)*0.5)
    for alpha in (0.3,0.6,0.85):
       add_pos(inits, 1.0 + alpha*(2.0*x-1.0))
       add_pos(inits, 1.0 - alpha*(2.0*x-1.0))
    # trig many
    for r in range(18):
       vals=np.ones(n)
       K=int(rng.integers(2,6))
       for c in range(K):
          f=int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n//8)))
          amp=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.35))
          ph=float(rng.uniform(-np.pi,np.pi))
          vals += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*x + ph)
          if r%2: vals += amp*np.sin(...)
       vals=make_pos(vals)
       add_pos(inits, vals)
    # lowpass
    Nf=1<<(n-1).bit_length()
    for r in range(8):
       f=np.fft.rfftfreq(Nf)*Nf
       alpha=float(rng.uniform(1.5,3.5))
       power=1.0/(np.abs(f)+1.0)**alpha
       power[0]=0
       phase=rng.uniform(-np.pi,np.pi,size=Nf//2+1)
       spec=np.zeros(Nf//2+1, complex); spec = power*np.exp(1j*phase); spec[0]=0
       noise=np.fft.irfft(spec, Nf)[:n]
       noise -= noise.mean()
       amp=float(rng.uniform(0.3,0.8))
       vals=1.0 + amp*noise
       # if vals min negative, scale amplitude down? make_pos clips; but clipping may bias. Use adjust:
       mn=float(np.min(vals))
       if mn < 0.05:
          # vals = 1 + amp*noise scaled so min 0.05
          scale=(1.0-0.05)/ (1.0 - mn) if 1-mn>0 else1
          vals = 1 + amp*noise*scale? Actually vals=1+amp*noise*scale.
       add_pos(vals)
    # sparse
    for frac in (0.5,0.65,0.8):
       mask=rng.random(n) < frac
       vals=np.zeros(n)
       vals[mask]=0.2+0.8*rng.random(int(mask.sum()))? Need random for all maybe
       vals=np.where(mask, 0.2+0.8*rng.random(n), 0.0)
       add_pos
    # endpoint
    for width in (0.05,0.1,0.2):
       vals=0.2 + 0.8*(np.exp(-((x/width)**2)) + np.exp(-(((1-x)/width)**2)))
       add_pos
    # block
    for nb in (8,16,32,64):
       block_vals=0.2+0.8*rng.random(nb)
       # smooth block_vals? add
       a=np.repeat(block_vals, n//nb)
       if len(a)<n: pad
       add_pos
    # random uniform
    for r in range(6):
       vals=0.1+0.9*rng.random(n)
       add_pos
    # If too many, cap random
    return inits
```

```
def perturb_best(a, rng, strength=None):
    p=normalize_a(a.copy())
    mode=int(rng.integers(0,5))
    n=len(p)
    if strength is None: strength=float(rng.uniform(0.2,0.7))
    if mode==0:
       noise=rng.standard_normal(n)*strength
       q=p*np.exp(noise)
    elif mode==1:
       q=p.copy()
       t=float(rng.uniform(0.1,0.5))
       q=(1.0-t)*p + t*(np.ones(n)/n)
    elif mode==2:
       q=p[::-1].copy()
       noise=rng.standard_normal(n)*0.1
       q=q*np.exp(noise)
    elif mode==3:
       # targeted: compute h from high d and apply
       d = ...? Use helper `pressure_direction(p)`
       h = pressure_direction(p, beta=100)
       scale = strength/(max(h.max(),1e-12))
       q=p*np.exp(-scale*h)
    else:
       q=p.copy()
       # random Fourier multiplication in log? Use add smooth noise to logp
       logp=np.log(p+1e-30)
       noise=lowpass_noise(n,rng,strength)
       logp += noise
       q=np.exp(logp); normalize
```
Need top-level `pressure_direction(p, beta)` returns h. But we can also avoid d for mode3? For perturbation not critical. Could simpler: q=p*np.exp(-strength*h) with h from top weights. Implement `direction_h(p, beta)` top-level.

```
sig=rng.standard_normal(n); sig=np.convolve(sig, np.ones(7)/7, mode='same'); sig-=sig.mean()
```

Now, inside `optimize_trajectory`, after main schedule, do perturb loop:

```
def finalize_perturbations(best_a, best_sc, deadline, rng):
   a=best_a; sc=best_sc

   return
```

Detailed function implementations:

```
def next_power_of_two(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
```

`fast_self_conv(a)`:
```
a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
n = int(a.size)
M = 2*n - 1
N = next_power_of_two(M)
A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
out = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:M]
out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
return out
```
If A*A overflows? Could, but a normalized sum1 values ≤1, safe. L-BFGS a maybe up to 10, c up to 1e5, square A up to? double okay. If a at bound 10 all, S=10n, d normalized small; c ~100n, A ~1e6, square 1e12 safe. Good.

`corr_pair(a,w)`:
```
a=np.asarray(a,float); w=np.asarray(w,float)
n=len(a)
M=n+len(w)-1
N=next_power_of_two(M)
A=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], N)
W=np.fft.rfft(w, N)
out=np.fft.irfft(A*W,N)[:M]
return out[n-1:2*n-1]
```
If w contains nan? no.

`normalize_a`:
```
def normalize_a(a, target=1.0):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,None)
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-12:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)*(target/a.size if a.size>0 else 1.0)
    a = a * (target / S)
    # if extremely small, return
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,None)
    S2=float(a.sum())
    if S2<1e-12: uniform
    return a*(target/S2)
```
If a.size 0? not.

`make_pos`: similar, but if sum zero uniform. Maybe same as normalize_a? Use `normalize_a` for init after clipping. But init raw may have sum zero; normalize. Use normalize.

`d_from_a`:
```
def d_from_a(a):
   n=len(a); S=float(a.sum())
   if S<1e-12: return np.zeros(2*n-1), S
   c=fast_self_conv(a)
   d=(float(n)*c)/(S*S)
   return d,S
```

`exact_score_a`:
```
def exact_score_a(a):
   n=len(a); S=float(a.sum())
   if S<1e-12: return np.inf
   c=fast_self_conv(a)
   cmax=float(np.max(c))
   if cmax<0: cmax=0
   return float(2.0 * n * cmax / (S*S))
```
Scale for p sum1: 2 max d. Good.

`estimate_maxfun`:
```
def estimate_maxfun(t_left, n):
    t_left = float(max(1.0, t_left))
    if t_left < 5.0:
        return 80
    base = 0.018
    scale = max(0.5, float(n)/8192.0)
    log_scale = max(1.0, np.log2(max(16,n)) / np.log2(8192.0))
    est = base * scale * log_scale
    mf = int((0.65 * t_left) / max(est, 1e-4))
    if mf < 80: mf=80
    if mf > 3000: mf=3000
    return mf
```
For n small 1024 scale=0.5 cap? Use max(0.25) maybe. If t_left 100, n=1024 est=0.018*0.5*~0.79=0.007; mf=9318 cap 3000. Good. If est too low and eval 0.01, call 30s. okay.

`run_lbfgs_tuple`:
```
def run_lbfgs_tuple(obj_func, a0, param, maxfun, deadline):
    a=normalize_a(a0)
    n=len(a)
    if deadline is not None:
       rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
       if rem < 5: return exact_score_a(a), a
       if maxfun is None: maxfun=estimate_maxfun(rem,n)
    else: maxfun=2000
    maxfun=int(maxfun)
    maxiter=int(max(50, maxfun//2))
    bounds=[(0.0, 10.0)] * n
    opts={'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':1e-12,'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':maxfun,'maxls':50}
    try:
       res = minimize(obj_func, a, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, args=(param,), bounds=bounds, options=opts)
    except Exception:
       return exact_score_a(a), a
    if hasattr(res,'x') and res.x is not None:
       a_out=normalize_a(res.x)
    else:
       a_out=a
    sc=exact_score_a(a_out)
    if not np.isfinite(sc) or sc>1e9:
       a_out=a; sc=exact_score_a(a)
    return sc,a_out
```
Need if `deadline` not None and maxfun not None? We pass often. Use parameter default None? Top-level can have default. No closures.
Potential issue: `obj_func` is `fun_lse_tuple` or `fun_topk_tuple`; args param. For topk param float but function int.

```
def run_mode(a0, mode, param, deadline, maxfun=None):
    if mode==0:
       return run_lbfgs_tuple(fun_lse_tuple, a0, float(param), maxfun, deadline)
    else:
       return run_lbfgs_tuple(fun_topk_tuple, a0, float(param), maxfun, deadline)
```

Implement robust.
```
def smooth_lowpass_noise(n, rng, strength):
    noise = rng.standard_normal(n)
    k=7
    kern=np.ones(k)/k
    noise=np.convolve(noise,kern,mode='same')
    noise -= noise.mean()
    return noise*strength
```
Top-level.

`perturb_best`:

```
def perturb_best(a, rng, min_strength=None, max_strength=None):
    p=normalize_a(a.copy())
    n=len(p)
    if min_strength is None: strength=float(rng.uniform(0.15,0.7))
    else strength
    mode=int(rng.integers(0,6))
    if mode==0:
       q=p*np.exp(strength*rng.standard_normal(n))
    elif mode==1:
       t=float(rng.uniform(0.1,0.5))
       q=(1-t)*p + t*(np.ones(n)/n)
    elif mode==2:
       q=p[::-1].copy()
       q=q*np.exp(0.1*rng.standard_normal(n))
    elif mode==3:
       h=pressure_direction(p, 100.0)
       hmax=float(np.max(np.abs(h)))
       if hmax<1e-12: q=p.copy()
       else: q=p*np.exp(-(strength*0.5/max(strength? no)) * h / hmax?)
```
For mode3, want exponent scale O(1): `scale = strength / max(h.max(), eps)`, q=p*exp(-scale*h). If h positive. If h all zero, p.
mode4:
```
       logp=np.log(p+1e-300)
       noise=smoother... maybe frequency
       logp += noise*strength
       q=np.exp(logp - logp.max())
```
mode5:
```
       # blur by convolution with gaussian kernel then renormalize
       k=int(rng.integers(5,15))|1
       kern=np.exp(-((np.arange(k)-(k//2))**2)/(2*(k//4+1)**2)); kern/=kern.sum()
       q=np.convolve(p,kern,mode='same')
```

Subgrad polish:
```
def subgrad_polish(a, max_iters=25, deadline=None):
   a=normalize_a(a.copy()); best_a=a; best_sc=exact; cur_sc=best_sc
   n=len(a)
   for it in range(max_iters):
      if deadline and time.monotonic()>deadline: break
      d,S=d_from_a(a)
      dmax=float(np.max(d)) if d.size else 0
      if not finite or dmax<=0: break
      e=np.exp(400*(d-dmax)); s=e.sum(); if s==0 break; e/=s
      h=corr_pair(a,e)
      hmax=float(np.max(h))
      if hmax<1e-12: break
      base=1.0/hmax
      best_candidate_a=a; best_candidate_sc=cur_sc
      for mult in (0.001,0.01,0.05,0.1,0.3,1.0):
          eta=mult*base
          q=a*np.exp(-eta*h)
          q=np.clip(q,0,None); S2=q.sum()
          if S2<1e-9: continue
          q=q*(1.0/S2)
          sc=exact_score_a(q)
          if sc < best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_a=q.copy()
          if sc < best_candidate_sc: best_candidate_sc=sc; best_candidate_a=q.copy()
      if best_candidate_a is None: break
      a=best_candidate_a; cur_sc=best_candidate_sc
      if time > deadline: break
   return best_sc,best_a
```
No closures. `best_candidate_a` initialized to a.copy? But if no improvement, continue same a. If no improvement, repeated same with same weights maybe no progress; could break if best_candidate_sc >= cur_sc? Actually best_candidate_sc initialized cur_sc, and q includes no improvement; a stays same. Next iteration same d => same, infinite. Need if no improvement break or perturb. Add:
```
if not (best_candidate_sc < cur_sc - 1e-12): break
```
But there might be tiny improvement. Use if `best_candidate_sc >= cur_sc - 1e-10: break`. Good.

Let's think about exact gradient sign and constant. If gradient constant large negative? At p uniform for n large, LSE beta high weights center; h at positions paired to center ~uniform 1/n, grad=4n*(1/n) -4*dmax =4 -3 =1 per element? For k center, h_j = a_{n-1-j}=1/n; grad=4 - 3 =1. Positive? Objective to minimize; gradient positive means increase a increases F? But center c=n-1 *2? Let's test p uniform n large. dmax≈1. Active center k=n-1. Decreasing a_j reduces c for all j? If we increase one a_j while maintaining sum (not here raw), center c increases by pairs; gradient positive if no sum constraint. On simplex, constant direction can be adjusted. Our raw gradient all positive ~1; line search with bounds, sum penalty zero at sum1. It may push a down to reduce sum? Since objective scale invariant exact, but LSE gradient positive along radial? For normalized LSE exact homogeneous, gradient radial should zero. Let's check our formula for LSE with d and p: w center, h_j=1/n, LDS=dmax=1. grad=4n*(1/n)/(S^2) -4*1/S =4 -4=0 for S=1. Wait I used h=corr_pair(a,w) = ∑w a_{k-j}; for center, h_j=a_{n-1-j}=1/n. `corr_pair` returns h; no factor 2. grad=4n*h/S² -4LDS/S =4n*(1/n)/1 -4*1=0. Good. For beta high and only center, uniform raw gradient zero (exact max has many? For uniform, center max, derivative along sum constraint? On full raw scale-invariant, radial gradient zero but tangential? If active center, gradient constant for raw? h_j=1/n constant, so gradient constant (after subtract LD) zero, so L-BFGS stuck at uniform! But uniform is not a local minimum for full max? Wait objective exact max: center coefficient c_{n-1}=∑ p_j p_{n-1-j}. Uniform has center max; derivative of center wrt p_j=2p_{n-1-j}=constant 2/n. On simplex, gradient constant -> uniform stationary for center alone. But by changing p (moving mass) while sum fixed, center may change second order; first-order no direction. So uniform is stationary? For exact max with single active, any distribution with p_j p_{n-1-j} pairs? If derivative constant, stationary for active set with one lag. Uniform is stationary of max? `resample_sequence` top-level:
```
def resample_sequence(a, n):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    m=a.size
    if m==0: return np.ones(n)/n
    if m==n: return a.copy()
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    return np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
```

`quick_optimize`:
```
def quick_optimize(a, deadline):
    a=normalize_a(a); best_a=a; best_sc=exact
    for beta in [300,800,2000,4000]:
       rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
       if rem < 12: break
       mf=estimate_maxfun(rem*0.7, len(a))
       if mf<80: break
       sc,a2=run_mode(a, beta, deadline, mf)
       update; a=normalize_a(a2)
    # one very high if time
    if time.monotonic() < deadline-10:
       rem=deadline-time-8
       if rem>10:
          mf=estimate_maxfun(rem,len(a)); sc,a2=run_mode(a,6000,deadline,mf)
    return best_sc,best_a
```
`run_mode` signature simpler: `run_mode(a0, beta, deadline, maxfun)`.

Implement:
```
def active_weights(d, k):
    m=d.size
    if k<=0 or k>=m: return np.ones(m)/m
    idx=np.argpartition(d, m-k)[m-k:]
    w=np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    w[idx]=1.0
    return w / float(k)
```

`subgrad_polish`:
```
def subgrad_polish(a, max_iters=20, deadline=None):
    a=normalize_a(a.copy())
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=exact; cur_sc=best_sc
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time > deadline: break
       d,S=d_from_a(a); dmax=...
       improved=False
       for k in (1,3,8):
          w=active_weights(d,k)
          h=corr_pair(a,w)
          hmax=float(np.max(h))
          if hmax<1e-12: continue
          base=1.0/hmax
          for mult in (0.02,0.08,0.2,0.5):
             eta=mult*base
             q=a*np.exp(-eta*h)
             q=normalize_a(q)
             sc=exact_score_a(q)
             if sc < best_sc: update
             if sc < cur_sc: cur_a=q; cur_sc=sc; improved=True
       if not improved: break
       a=cur_a
    return best_sc,best_a
```

So it prefers concentration over flat when max same? This is bad for small β: it doesn't represent max, it approximates max plus entropy term, and the logm/β constant makes it larger for flat if max equal. But if max differs, it may prefer lower max but penalizes flatness. Centering subtracts logm/β; L for peaked 0.75-1.03/4=0.493, flat 0.5. Still prefers peaked by 0.007. For β small, centered LSE approximates **mean of max?** Let's expand: e=1+β(d-mbar?) no, shifted by max; log(sume)≈logm + β(∑(d-max))/m? L≈max + (log m/β) + mean(d-max) - logm/β = mean(d). Actually subtracting logm gives mean approx, not peaked. My numerical β=4 not small. For β=4, peaked mean? d mean= (0.75+0.5*7000)/7001=0.500036. Peak surrogate 0.493 below mean, due nonlinearity? Maybe because max high makes many exp small? Hmm.

This suggests our centered LSE may not be monotonic in max for small/mid β and could encourage concentration at a single lag! That's dangerous. Starting from uniform, β=4 might move to make a single lag higher? But exact we don't use objective directly; L-BFGS minimizes surrogate. It might increase max at low beta before high beta. Continuation to high beta should fix, but low beta stage could harm. Standard LSE with β>0 is convex in d but not a monotone surrogate for max (it is ≥ max, but not tight; minimizing LSE may not minimize max). Actually for any β>0, LSE(d) ≥ max d, but it also has bias large when values close? Minimizing LSE could reduce max but also may prefer spread? Wait standard LSE is monotone componentwise: increasing any d increases LSE. So it penalizes high d more if already high? It is a smooth upper bound: LSE ≥ max. If you reduce max but keep others same, LSE decreases. But for two vectors: one with max 0.5 all equal, LSE=0.5+logm/β high; another with max 0.75 one high, others low, LSE=0.75+small. The latter LSE can be lower despite higher max because constant logm/β for flat. So LSE is not a faithful upper bound for max without the log? Standard LSE is ≥ max, and for flat all equal 0.5 LSE > max; but for max 0.75, LSE >0.75 but could be less than 0.5+constant. So it is not a scale-invariant proxy. Usually inLogSumExp tangents, use with large beta to approximate max. At low beta it's not a minimax approximation; it may prefer concentrating on one term. For our optimization from uniform to reduce max concentration, low beta might first increase a peak? That could be bad, but continuation to high beta may overcome if time. """Docstring..."""
```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

PEN_SUM = 1e-3
```

`add_to_inits`:
```
def add_to_inits(inits, arr, cap):
    if len(inits) >= cap: return
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0: return
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    mn = float(arr.min())
    if mn < 0.0:
        arr = arr - mn + 0.05
    p = normalize_a(arr)
    inits.append(p)
```

`generate_inits` uses add_to_inits. Need `parent` can be None. The `resample_sequence` for parent returns nonnegative if parent nonnegative. Good.

```
def generate_inits(n, rng, parent, cap=None):
    if cap is None: cap=24 if n>6000 else 32
    inits=[]
    if parent is not None and parent.size:
       pr=resample_sequence(parent,n)
       add_to_inits(inits, pr, cap)
       add_to_inits(inits, pr[::-1], cap)
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    add_to_inits(inits, np.ones(n), cap)
    for alpha in (0.3,0.6,0.85):
       add_to_inits(inits, 1.0 + alpha*(2.0*x-1.0), cap)
       add_to_inits(inits, 1.0 - alpha*(2.0*x-1.0), cap)
    max_trig=10 if n>6000 else 14
    for r in range(max_trig):
       vals=np.ones(n)
       K=int(rng.integers(2,6))
       for c in range(K):
          f=int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n//8)))
          amp=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.35))
          ph=float(rng.uniform(-np.pi,np.pi))
          vals += amp*np.cos(2.0*np.pi*f*x + ph)
          if r % 2 == 1:
             vals += amp*0.5*np.sin(2.0*np.pi*f*x + ph)
       add_to_inits(inits, vals, cap)
    max_low=5 if n>6000 else 8
    Nf=1 << (n-1).bit_length()
    for r in range(max_low):
       freqs = np.fft.rfftfreq(Nf)*Nf
       alpha_freq=float(rng.uniform(1.5,3.5))
       power=1.0/((np.abs(freqs)+1.0)**alpha_freq)
       if power.size: power[0]=0.0
       phase=np.empty(len(power)); phase[:] = rng.uniform(-np.pi,np.pi,size=len(power))
       spec=power*np.exp(1j*phase)
       spec[0]=0.0
       noise=np.fft.irfft(spec, Nf)[:n]
       noise=noise - float(noise.mean())
       amp=float(rng.uniform(0.3,0.9))
       vals=1.0 + amp*noise
       add_to_inits(inits, vals, cap)
    for frac in (0.5,0.65,0.8):
       mask=rng.random(n) < frac
       vals=np.where(mask, 0.2+0.8*rng.random(n), 0.0)
       add_to_inits(inits, vals, cap)
    for width in (0.05,0.1,0.2):
       vals=0.2 + 0.8*(np.exp(-((x/width)**2)) + np.exp(-(((1.0-x)/width)**2)))
       add_to_inits(inits, vals, cap)
    for nb in (8,16,32,64):
       if nb > n: nb = n
       block_vals=0.1+0.9*rng.random(nb)
       per=max(1, n//nb)
       a=np.repeat(block_vals, per)
       if a.size < n:
          a=np.concatenate([a, np.repeat(block_vals, n-a.size)])
       add_to_inits(inits, a[:n], cap)
    for r in range(5):
       add_to_inits(inits, 0.1+0.9*rng.random(n), cap)
    return inits
```
Potential issue `np.fft.rfftfreq(Nf)*Nf` frequency 0 and max. `phase` generation `np.empty` then assign. Good.
`n//8` could be 1 if n<8; max(2,). Fine.
`width` division by zero? width >0.
Endpoint formula double peaks sum max 1.8*0.8+0.2 =1.64, okay.

`smooth_noise`:
```
def smooth_noise(n,rng,strength):
    noise=rng.standard_normal(n)
    k=min(31,max(3,n//200))
    if k %2==0: k+=1
    if k>n: k = n if n%2==1 else max(3,n-1)
    if k>=3:
       center=k//2
       sig=max(1.0, center/2.0)
       kern=np.exp(-((np.arange(k)-center)**2)/(2.0*sig*sig))
       kern /= kern.sum()
       noise=np.convolve(noise,kern,mode='same')
    noise-=noise.mean()
    return noise*strength
```
If n=3, k=3 center1 sig0.5? max(1,0.5)=1. okay.

`pressure_direction`:
```
def pressure_direction(p, k=3):
    d,S=d_from_a(p)
    if S < 1e-12 or d.size==0: return np.zeros(len(p))
    dmax=float(np.max(d))
    if not np.isfinite(dmax) or dmax <=0: return np.zeros(len(p))
    w=active_weights(d,k)
    return corr_pair(p,w)
```

`active_weights` as above.

`perturb_best`:
```
def perturb_best(a, rng, strength=None):
    p=normalize_a(a.copy())
    n=len(p)
    if strength is None: strength=float(rng.uniform(0.15,0.7))
    mode=int(rng.integers(0,6))
    if mode == 0:
       q=p*np.exp(strength*rng.standard_normal(n))
    elif mode == 1:
       t=float(rng.uniform(0.1,0.5))
       q=(1.0-t)*p + t*(np.ones(n)/n)
    elif mode == 2:
       q=p[::-1].copy()
       q=q*np.exp(0.1*rng.standard_normal(n))
    elif mode == 3:
       h=pressure_direction(p, 4)
       hmax=float(np.max(h))
       if hmax < 1e-12:
          q=p.copy()
       else:
          q=p*np.exp(-strength*h/hmax)
    elif mode == 4:
       logp=np.log(p+1e-300)
       logp += smooth_noise(n, rng, strength*0.5)
       q=np.exp(logp - float(np.max(logp)))
    else:
       k=int(rng.integers(3,15))
       if k % 2 == 0: k += 1
       if k > n: k = n if n%2==1 else max(3,n-1)
       if k < 3: k=3
       center=k//2
       sig=max(1.0, center/2.0)
       kern=np.exp(-((np.arange(k)-center)**2)/(2.0*sig*sig))
       if kern.sum()>0: kern/=kern.sum()
       q=np.convolve(p,kern,mode='same')
    return normalize_a(q)
```
Need if `n` maybe 0, but no. If q all zeros due log underflow? `exp(logp-max)` sum maybe finite; if p zeros log-700, exp maybe 0; sum positive if some p. Good.

`subgrad_polish`: Use `active_weights`. Need `cur_a` variable.
```
def subgrad_polish(a, max_iters=20, deadline=None):
    a=normalize_a(a.copy())
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=exact_score_a(a)
    cur_sc=best_sc
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       d,S=d_from_a(a)
       if S < 1e-12 or d.size==0: break
       dmax=float(np.max(d))
       if not np.isfinite(dmax) or dmax <=0: break
       cur_a=None; cur_new_sc=cur_sc
       for k in (1,3,8):
          w=active_weights(d,k)
          h=corr_pair(a,w)
          hmax=float(np.max(h))
          if hmax < 1e-12: continue
          base=1.0/hmax
          for mult in (0.02,0.08,0.2,0.5):
             if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline: break
             eta=mult*base
             q=a*np.exp(-eta*h)
             q=normalize_a(q)
             sc=exact_score_a(q)
             if np.isfinite(sc):
                if sc < best_sc:
                   best_sc=sc; best_a=q.copy()
                if sc < cur_new_sc:
                   cur_new_sc=sc; cur_a=q.copy()
       if cur_a is None: break
       a=cur_a; cur_sc=cur_new_sc
    return float(best_sc), best_a
```
Good.

```
def quick_optimize(a, deadline):
   a=normalize_a(a.copy())
   best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=exact_score_a(a)
   for beta in (400,1200,4000,10000):
      rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
      if rem < 15: break
      mf=estimate_maxfun(rem*0.8, len(a))
      if mf < 64: break
      sc,a2=run_mode(a, beta, deadline, mf)
      if sc < best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_a=a2.copy()
      a=normalize_a(a2)
   rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
   if rem > 20:
      mf=estimate_maxfun(rem*0.8, len(a))
      if mf >=64:
         sc,a2=run_mode(a, 20000, deadline, mf)
         if sc < best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_a=a2.copy()
   return float(best_sc), best_a
```

```
def optimize_trajectory(a_start, deadline, rng, label):
    a=normalize_a(a_start)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=exact_score_a(a)
    print(f"[{label}] start score={best_sc:.8f} n={len(a)}")
    t0=time.monotonic()
    total = max(10.0, deadline - t0 - 15.0)
    stages = [(50,0.08),...,(4000,0.08)]
    cum=0.0
    n=len(a)
    for beta,frac in stages:
       cum += frac
       sub_dead = t0 + cum*total
       sub_dead = min(sub_dead, deadline - 5.0)
       while time.monotonic() < sub_dead - 2.0:
          rem = sub_dead - time.monotonic()
          if rem < 6.0: break
          mf=estimate_maxfun(rem*0.8, n)
          if mf < 64: break
          sc,a2=run_mode(a, float(beta), deadline, mf)
          if sc < best_sc:
             best_sc=sc; best_a=a2.copy(); print...
          a=normalize_a(a2)
    # final high if time
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 15:
       for beta in (6000,):
          rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
          if rem<20: break
          mf=estimate_maxfun(rem*0.8,n)
          sc,a2=run_mode(a,float(beta),deadline,mf)
          update
    return float(best_sc), best_a
```

`estimate_eval_time`:
```
def estimate_eval_time(n):
    base=0.018
    scale=max(0.5, float(n)/8192.0)
    logscale=max(1.0, float(np.log2(max(16,n)))/float(np.log2(8192.0)))
    return base*scale*logscale
```
`estimate_maxfun`:
```
def estimate_maxfun(t_left,n):
    t_left=float(max(1.0,t_left))
    if t_left < 4.0: return 64
    est=estimate_eval_time(n)
    mf=int((0.7*t_left)/max(est,1e-4))
    if mf<64: mf=64
    if mf>3000: mf=3000
    return mf
```
In run, cap with 0.9.

`run_lbfgs_tuple`:
```
def run_lbfgs_tuple(obj_func, a0, param, deadline, maxfun):
    a=normalize_a(a0)
    n=len(a)
    if deadline is not None:
       rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
       if rem < 4.0: return exact_score_a(a), a
       est=estimate_eval_time(n)
       cap=int((0.9*rem)/max(est,1e-4))
       if cap < 64: cap=64
       maxfun=int(min(int(maxfun), cap))
    else:
       maxfun=int(maxfun) if maxfun is not None else 1000
    if maxfun < 64: maxfun=64
    maxiter=max(50, maxfun//2)
    bounds=[(0.0,10.0)]*n
    opts={'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':1e-12,'maxiter':int(maxiter),'maxfun':int(maxfun),'maxls':50}
    try:
       res=minimize(obj_func, a, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, args=(param,), bounds=bounds, options=opts)
    except Exception:
       return exact_score_a(a), a
    a_out=normalize_a(res.x) if res.x is not None else a
    sc=exact_score_a(a_out)
    if not np.isfinite(sc) or sc > 1e8:
       a_out=a; sc=exact_score_a(a)
    return float(sc), a_out
```
What if `res.x` has tiny negative? normalize clips.

```
best_seq=normalize_a(best_seq)

   mf=estimate_maxfun(rem*0.8,len(best_seq))
   sc,seq=run_mode(best_seq,8000,final_deadline,mf); update
   if time<final_deadline-25:
      mf=estimate_maxfun((final_deadline-time)*0.8,len(best_seq))
      sc,seq=run_mode(best_seq,20000,final_deadline,mf); update
# subgrad small
if time<final_deadline-15:
   sc,seq=subgrad_polish(best_seq, max_iters=6, deadline=final_deadline-15); update
```

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative sequence minimizing

    2 * n * max(conv(a, a)) / sum(a)^2

using:
- FFT-based exact scoring and LSE-smooth objectives on scaled coefficients,
- continuation in LSE temperature,
- L-BFGS-B in the original nonnegative variables with a small sum penalty,
- population-like starts from the parent, resampled lengths, and diverse inits,
- exact-subgradient multiplicative pressure moves,
- random multiplicative/log perturbation restarts in the final phase.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

PEN_SUM = 1e-3


def next_power_of_two(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def fast_self_conv(a):
    """Full linear self-convolution via FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    M = 2 * n - 1
    N = next_power_of_two(M)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    out = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:M]
    return np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)


def corr_pair(a, w):
    """
    h_j = sum_k w_k * a_{k-j}, for j = 0,...,n-1,
    computed as a middle slice of conv(a[::-1], w).
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0 or w.size == 0:
        return np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    M = n + int(w.size) - 1
    N = next_power_of_two(M)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], N)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    full = np.fft.irfft(A * W, N)[:M]
    return full[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def normalize_a(a, target=1.0):
    """Clip to non-negative, normalize to target sum."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-12:
        return np.full(a.size, float(target) / float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    a = a * (float(target) / S)
    S2 = float(a.sum())
    if S2 < 1e-12:
        return np.full(a.size, float(target) / float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return a * (float(target) / S2)


def resample_sequence(a, n):
    """Linearly resample a non-negative sequence to length n."""
    a = np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel(), nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    m = int(a.size)
    if m == 0:
        return np.ones(int(n), dtype=np.float64) / float(max(1, n))
    if m == int(n):
        return a.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(n))
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, a).astype(np.float64)


def d_from_a(a):
    """
    d = n * conv(a,a) / sum(a)^2.
    The exact score is 2 * max(d).
    """
    n = len(a)
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S < 1e-12:
        return np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64), S
    c = fast_self_conv(a)
    d = (float(n) * c) / (S * S)
    d = np.maximum(d, 0.0)
    return d, S


def exact_score_a(a):
    """Exact score 2*n*max(conv(a,a))/sum(a)^2."""
    n = len(a)
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S < 1e-12:
        return np.inf
    c = fast_self_conv(a)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if cmax < 0.0:
        cmax = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * cmax / (S * S))


def lse_score_grad_tuple(a, beta):
    """
    LSE-smooth objective and gradient for d = n*conv(a,a)/sum(a)^2.
    Objective:
        F = 2 * LSE_beta(d) + PEN * (sum(a)-1)^2
    Gradient is exact for this objective.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-8:
        return 1e10, grad

    beta = float(beta)
    if not np.isfinite(beta) or beta <= 1e-6:
        beta = 100.0

    d, S = d_from_a(a)
    dmax = float(np.max(d))
    if not np.isfinite(dmax) or dmax <= 0.0:
        return 1e10, grad

    z = beta * (d - dmax)
    e = np.exp(z)
    sum_e = float(e.sum())
    if sum_e <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(sum_e):
        return 1e10, grad
    w = e / sum_e

    L = dmax + np.log(sum_e) / beta
    LDS = float(np.dot(w, d))

    h = corr_pair(a, w)
    score = 2.0 * L + PEN_SUM * (S - 1.0) * (S - 1.0)

    grad = (4.0 * float(n) * h) / (S * S)
    grad -= (4.0 * LDS) / S
    grad += 2.0 * PEN_SUM * (S - 1.0)

    grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    grad = np.clip(grad, -1e6, 1e6)
    return float(score), grad


def fun_lse_tuple(a, beta):
    return lse_score_grad_tuple(a, beta)


def estimate_eval_time(n):
    """Conservative estimate of seconds per LSE objective+gradient evaluation."""
    base = 0.018
    scale = max(0.5, float(n) / 8192.0)
    log_scale = max(1.0, float(np.log2(max(16, n))) / float(np.log2(8192.0)))
    return base * scale * log_scale


def estimate_maxfun(t_left, n):
    t_left = float(max(1.0, t_left))
    if t_left < 4.0:
        return 64
    est = estimate_eval_time(n)
    mf = int((0.7 * t_left) / max(est, 1e-4))
    if mf < 64:
        mf = 64
    if mf > 3000:
        mf = 3000
    return mf


def run_lbfgs_tuple(obj_func, a0, param, deadline, maxfun):
    """
    Run L-BFGS-B with an objective/gradient function returning (value, grad).
    """
    a = normalize_a(a0)
    n = len(a)
    if deadline is not None:
        rem = float(deadline - time.monotonic())
        if rem < 4.0:
            return exact_score_a(a), a
        est = estimate_eval_time(n)
        cap = int((0.9 * rem) / max(est, 1e-4))
        if cap < 64:
            cap = 64
        maxfun = int(min(int(maxfun), cap))
    else:
        maxfun = int(maxfun if maxfun is not None else 1000)

    if maxfun < 64:
        maxfun = 64
    maxiter = max(50, maxfun // 2)
    bounds = [(0.0, 10.0)] * n
    opts = {
        "ftol": 1e-15,
        "gtol": 1e-12,
        "maxiter": int(maxiter),
        "maxfun": int(maxfun),
        "maxls": 50,
    }

    try:
        res = minimize(
            obj_func,
            a,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            args=(param,),
            bounds=bounds,
            options=opts,
        )
    except Exception:
        return exact_score_a(a), a

    if getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
        a_out = normalize_a(res.x)
    else:
        a_out = a

    sc = exact_score_a(a_out)
    if not np.isfinite(sc) or sc > 1e8:
        a_out = a
        sc = exact_score_a(a)
    return float(sc), a_out


def run_mode(a0, beta, deadline, maxfun):
    return run_lbfgs_tuple(fun_lse_tuple, a0, float(beta), deadline, maxfun)


def add_to_inits(inits, arr, cap):
    if len(inits) >= cap:
        return
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    mn = float(np.min(arr))
    if mn < 0.0:
        arr = arr - mn + 0.05
    p = normalize_a(arr)
    inits.append(p)


def generate_inits(n, rng, parent, cap=None):
    if cap is None:
        cap = 24 if n > 6000 else 32
    inits = []
    n = int(n)

    if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
        pr = resample_sequence(parent, n)
        add_to_inits(inits, pr, cap)
        add_to_inits(inits, pr[::-1], cap)

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    add_to_inits(inits, np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), cap)

    for alpha in (0.3, 0.6, 0.85):
        add_to_inits(inits, 1.0 + alpha * (2.0 * x - 1.0), cap)
        add_to_inits(inits, 1.0 - alpha * (2.0 * x - 1.0), cap)

    max_trig = 10 if n > 6000 else 14
    for r in range(max_trig):
        vals = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        K = int(rng.integers(2, 6))
        for _ in range(K):
            f = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n // 8)))
            amp = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.35))
            ph = float(rng.uniform(-np.pi, np.pi))
            vals += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * x + ph)
            if r % 2 == 1:
                vals += 0.5 * amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * x + ph)
        add_to_inits(inits, vals, cap)

    max_low = 5 if n > 6000 else 8
    Nf = 1 << (n - 1).bit_length()
    for _ in range(max_low):
        freqs = np.fft.rfftfreq(Nf) * Nf
        power = 1.0 / ((np.abs(freqs) + 1.0) ** float(rng.uniform(1.5, 3.5)))
        power[0] = 0.0
        phase = rng.uniform(-np.pi, np.pi, size=len(power))
        spec = power * np.exp(1j * phase)
        spec[0] = 0.0
        noise = np.fft.irfft(spec, Nf)[:n]
        noise = noise - float(noise.mean())
        vals = 1.0 + float(rng.uniform(0.3, 0.9)) * noise
        add_to_inits(inits, vals, cap)

    for frac in (0.50, 0.65, 0.80):
        mask = rng.random(n) < frac
        vals = np.where(mask, 0.2 + 0.8 * rng.random(n), 0.0)
        add_to_inits(inits, vals, cap)

    for width in (0.05, 0.10, 0.20):
        vals = 0.2 + 0.8 * (
            np.exp(-((x / width) ** 2)) + np.exp(-(((1.0 - x) / width) ** 2))
        )
        add_to_inits(inits, vals, cap)

    for nb in (8, 16, 32, 64):
        if nb > n:
            nb = n
        block_vals = 0.1 + 0.9 * rng.random(nb)
        per = max(1, n // nb)
        a = np.repeat(block_vals, per)
        if a.size < n:
            a = np.concatenate([a, np.repeat(block_vals, n - a.size)])
        add_to_inits(inits, a[:n], cap)

    for _ in range(5):
        add_to_inits(inits, 0.1 + 0.9 * rng.random(n), cap)

    return inits


def active_weights(d, k):
    m = int(d.size)
    if m <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if k <= 0 or k >= m:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    idx = np.argpartition(d, m - k)[m - k:]
    w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    w[idx] = 1.0
    return w / float(k)


def pressure_direction(p, k=3):
    d, S = d_from_a(p)
    n = len(p)
    if S < 1e-12 or d.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    dmax = float(np.max(d))
    if not np.isfinite(dmax) or dmax <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    w = active_weights(d, k)
    return corr_pair(p, w)


def smooth_noise(n, rng, strength):
    noise = rng.standard_normal(int(n))
    k = min(31, max(3, int(n) // 200))
    if k % 2 == 0:
        k += 1
    if k > int(n):
        k = int(n) if int(n) % 2 == 1 else max(3, int(n) - 1)
    if k >= 3:
        c = k // 2
        sig = max(1.0, c / 2.0)
        kern = np.exp(-((np.arange(k) - c) ** 2) / (2.0 * sig * sig))
        kern = kern / float(kern.sum())
        noise = np.convolve(noise, kern, mode="same")
    noise = noise - float(noise.mean())
    return noise * float(strength)


def perturb_best(a, rng, strength=None):
    p = normalize_a(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel())
    n = len(p)
    if n == 0:
        return p
    if strength is None:
        strength = float(rng.uniform(0.15, 0.70))
    mode = int(rng.integers(0, 6))

    if mode == 0:
        q = p * np.exp(strength * rng.standard_normal(n))
    elif mode == 1:
        t = float(rng.uniform(0.10, 0.50))
        q = (1.0 - t) * p + t * (np.ones(n) / float(n))
    elif mode == 2:
        q = p[::-1].copy()
        q = q * np.exp(0.1 * rng.standard_normal(n))
    elif mode == 3:
        h = pressure_direction(p, 4)
        hmax = float(np.max(h))
        if hmax < 1e-12:
            q = p.copy()
        else:
            q = p * np.exp(-strength * h / hmax)
    elif mode == 4:
        logp = np.log(p + 1e-300)
        logp = logp + smooth_noise(n, rng, strength * 0.5)
        q = np.exp(logp - float(np.max(logp)))
    else:
        k = int(rng.integers(3, 15))
        if k % 2 == 0:
            k += 1
        if k > n:
            k = n if n % 2 == 1 else max(3, n - 1)
        if k < 3:
            k = 3
        c = k // 2
        sig = max(1.0, c / 2.0)
        kern = np.exp(-((np.arange(k) - c) ** 2) / (2.0 * sig * sig))
        knorm = float(kern.sum())
        if knorm > 0.0:
            kern = kern / knorm
        q = np.convolve(p, kern, mode="same")

    return normalize_a(q)


def quick_optimize(a, deadline):
    a = normalize_a(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score_a(a)

    for beta in (400, 1200, 4000, 10000):
        rem = float(deadline - time.monotonic())
        if rem < 15.0:
            break
        mf = estimate_maxfun(rem * 0.8, len(a))
        if mf < 64:
            break
        sc, a2 = run_mode(a, float(beta), deadline, mf)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a2.copy()
        a = normalize_a(a2)

    rem = float(deadline - time.monotonic())
    if rem > 20.0:
        mf = estimate_maxfun(rem * 0.8, len(a))
        if mf >= 64:
            sc, a2 = run_mode(a, 20000.0, deadline, mf)
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = a2.copy()

    return float(best_sc), best_a


def optimize_trajectory(a_start, deadline, rng, label):
    a = normalize_a(a_start)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score_a(a)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total = max(10.0, float(deadline - t0 - 15.0))
    n = len(a)

    print("[{}] start score={:.8f} n={}".format(label, best_sc, n))

    stages = [
        (50.0, 0.08),
        (100.0, 0.10),
        (150.0, 0.12),
        (250.0, 0.12),
        (400.0, 0.12),
        (600.0, 0.12),
        (1000.0, 0.14),
        (2000.0, 0.12),
        (4000.0, 0.08),
    ]

    cum = 0.0
    for beta, frac in stages:
        cum += frac
        sub_dead = t0 + cum * total
        sub_dead = min(float(sub_dead), float(deadline - 5.0))

        while time.monotonic() < sub_dead - 2.0:
            rem = float(sub_dead - time.monotonic())
            if rem < 6.0:
                break
            mf = estimate_maxfun(rem * 0.8, n)
            if mf < 64:
                break
            sc, a2 = run_mode(a, beta, deadline, mf)
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = a2.copy()
                print("[{}] new best {:.8f} beta={:.0f} elapsed={:.0f}s".format(
                    label, best_sc, beta, time.monotonic() - t0
                ))
            a = normalize_a(a2)

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 15.0:
        rem = float(deadline - time.monotonic())
        if rem > 20.0:
            mf = estimate_maxfun(rem * 0.8, n)
            if mf >= 64:
                sc, a2 = run_mode(a, 6000.0, deadline, mf)
                if sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best_a = a2.copy()
                    print("[{}] final high beta {:.8f}".format(label, best_sc))

    return float(best_sc), best_a


def subgrad_polish(a, max_iters=20, deadline=None):
    a = normalize_a(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score_a(a)
    cur_sc = best_sc

    for _ in range(int(max_iters)):
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > float(deadline):
            break
        d, S = d_from_a(a)
        if S < 1e-12 or d.size == 0:
            break
        dmax = float(np.max(d))
        if not np.isfinite(dmax) or dmax <= 0.0:
            break

        cur_a = None
        cur_new_sc = cur_sc

        for k in (1, 3, 8):
            w = active_weights(d, k)
            h = corr_pair(a, w)
            hmax = float(np.max(h))
            if hmax < 1e-12:
                continue
            base = 1.0 / hmax
            for mult in (0.02, 0.08, 0.20, 0.50):
                if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > float(deadline):
                    break
                eta = mult * base
                q = a * np.exp(-eta * h)
                q = normalize_a(q)
                sc = exact_score_a(q)
                if not np.isfinite(sc):
                    continue
                if sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best_a = q.copy()
                if sc < cur_new_sc:
                    cur_new_sc = sc
                    cur_a = q.copy()

        if cur_a is None:
            break
        a = cur_a
        cur_sc = cur_new_sc

    return float(best_sc), best_a


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """Search for a low-score nonnegative sequence under the 2400s budget."""
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = float(budget_s)
    deadline = t_start + total_budget - 15.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    parent = None
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if h is not None:
            p = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if p.size >= 64:
                parent = p
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    lengths = []
    parent_len = 0
    if parent is not None:
        parent_len = int(min(parent.size, 12288))
        if parent_len < 1024:
            parent_len = 1024
        lengths.append(parent_len)
        if parent_len < 8192:
            lengths.append(8192)
        if parent_len > 4096:
            lengths.append(4096)
    else:
        lengths.extend([8192, 4096])

    unique_lengths = []
    for n in lengths:
        n = int(n)
        if 1024 <= n <= 12288 and n not in unique_lengths:
            unique_lengths.append(n)
    if not unique_lengths:
        unique_lengths = [8192]
    lengths = unique_lengths[:3]

    print("Parent available: {}, length={}".format(parent is not None, parent.size if parent is not None else 0))
    print("Lengths to try: {}".format(lengths))

    all_scores = []
    all_seqs = []
    all_lens = []

    for n in lengths:
        inits = generate_inits(n, rng, parent)
        for arr in inits:
            p = normalize_a(arr)
            sc = exact_score_a(p)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                all_scores.append(float(sc))
                all_seqs.append(p)
                all_lens.append(int(n))

    if not all_scores:
        n0 = lengths[0]
        all_seqs.append(np.ones(n0, dtype=np.float64) / float(n0))
        all_scores.append(float(exact_score_a(all_seqs[-1])))
        all_lens.append(n0)

    order = np.argsort(np.asarray(all_scores, dtype=np.float64))

    starts = []
    used_lens = set()

    if parent is not None and parent_len > 0:
        ps = normalize_a(resample_sequence(parent, parent_len))
        starts.append(ps)
        used_lens.add(parent_len)

    if len(starts) == 0:
        idx0 = int(order[0])
        starts.append(all_seqs[idx0].copy())
        used_lens.add(int(all_lens[idx0]))

    second_added = False
    for idx in order:
        nidx = int(all_lens[idx])
        if nidx not in used_lens:
            starts.append(all_seqs[int(idx)].copy())
            used_lens.add(nidx)
            second_added = True
            break
    if not second_added:
        starts.append(perturb_best(starts[0], rng))

    l1 = len(starts[0])
    l2 = len(starts[1])
    third_added = False
    for idx in order:
        nidx = int(all_lens[idx])
        if nidx not in (l1, l2):
            starts.append(all_seqs[int(idx)].copy())
            third_added = True
            break
    if not third_added:
        starts.append(perturb_best(starts[0], rng))

    starts = starts[:3]

    best_seq = starts[0].copy()
    best_score = exact_score_a(best_seq)
    for i in range(1, len(starts)):
        sc = exact_score_a(starts[i])
        if sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_seq = starts[i].copy()

    print("Initial best score={:.8f} length={}".format(best_score, len(best_seq)))

    main_budget = max(120.0, deadline - t_start - 240.0)
    if len(starts) == 1:
        fr = [1.00]
    elif len(starts) == 2:
        fr = [0.62, 0.38]
    else:
        fr = [0.55, 0.30, 0.15]

    cum = 0.0
    for i, st in enumerate(starts):
        cum += fr[i]
        sub_dead = t_start + cum * main_budget
        sub_dead = min(float(sub_dead), float(deadline - 240.0))
        if time.monotonic() > max(t_start + 10.0, sub_dead - 30.0):
            continue

        sq = st
        if i > 0 and np.isfinite(best_score) and best_score < 2.0:
            base = best_seq
            if len(base) != len(sq):
                base = resample_sequence(base, len(sq))
            base = normalize_a(base)
            if i == 1:
                sq = base
            else:
                sq = perturb_best(base, rng)

        sc, seq = optimize_trajectory(sq, sub_dead, rng, "traj{}".format(i + 1))
        if sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_seq = seq.copy()
            print("Global best after traj{}: {:.8f}".format(i + 1, best_score))

    best_seq = normalize_a(best_seq)

    # Final exact-subgradient pressure moves.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 90.0:
        sc, seq = subgrad_polish(best_seq, max_iters=18, deadline=deadline - 90.0)
        if sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_seq = seq.copy()
            print("Subgradient polish best: {:.8f}".format(best_score))

    # Final perturbation restarts.
    perturb_done = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 100.0 and perturb_done < 5:
        left = float(deadline - 100.0 - time.monotonic())
        if left < 90.0:
            break
        chunk = time.monotonic() + left / float(5 - perturb_done)
        p = perturb_best(best_seq, rng)
        sc, seq = quick_optimize(p, chunk)
        if sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_seq = seq.copy()
            print("Perturb restart best: {:.8f}".format(best_score))
        perturb_done += 1

    # Very high-temperature LSE polish on the best sequence.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 40.0:
        rem = float(deadline - time.monotonic() - 5.0)
        if rem > 10.0:
            mf = estimate_maxfun(rem * 0.8, len(best_seq))
            sc, seq = run_mode(best_seq, 8000.0, deadline, mf)
            if sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_seq = seq.copy()
                print("High beta final best: {:.8f}".format(best_score))

            if time.monotonic() < deadline - 25.0:
                rem = float(deadline - time.monotonic() - 5.0)
                if rem > 10.0:
                    mf = estimate_maxfun(rem * 0.8, len(best_seq))
                    sc, seq = run_mode(best_seq, 20000.0, deadline, mf)
                    if sc < best_score:
                        best_score = sc
                        best_seq = seq.copy()
                        print("Very high beta final best: {:.8f}".format(best_score))

    # Small final exact-subgradient cleanup.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 15.0:
        sc, seq = subgrad_polish(best_seq, max_iters=6, deadline=deadline - 15.0)
        if sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_seq = seq.copy()
            print("Final subgradient best: {:.8f}".format(best_score))

    out_arr = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    out_arr = np.nan_to_num(out_arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(out_arr.sum())
    if S < 0.01:
        out_arr = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) * 0.5
    else:
        out_arr = out_arr / S
        out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)

    out = [float(x) for x in out_arr]
    if sum(out) < 0.01:
        out = [0.5] * 64

    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
    except Exception:
        verified = best_score

    print("FINAL internal best={:.8f} verified={:.8f} length={} time={:.1f}s".format(
        best_score, verified, len(out), time.monotonic() - t_start
    ))
    return out
```
